# PRD Section Aggregate — structured returns from the 15 feature-section authors

> Machine-collected. Each feature author returned these while writing its 7.N subsection.

> Counts: 291 glossary terms, 187 external-tech rows, 76 data entities, 199 open questions,
> 196 candidate product-wide rules, 455 quirks, 1354 functional requirements.


---

## Glossary terms

- **term**: Model backend / provider | **definition**: A pluggable implementation of the five-operation provider contract that turns a conversation into a reply. Three ship: two hosted cloud backends and one local-machine backend. | **sourceNames**: IAIService, AzureOpenAIService, BedrockService, LLamaSharpService, _aiServices dictionary
- **term**: Provider registry | **definition**: The name-keyed table the shell holds mapping the lower-case provider names 'azure', 'bedrock', 'llama' to backend instances. | **sourceNames**: Dictionary<string, IAIService> _aiServices in both shells
- **term**: Token log probabilities / per-token confidence data | **definition**: Per-token natural-log probability values returned alongside a reply, each optionally carrying ranked alternative tokens. Displayed as a percentage confidence computed as e^(log probability). | **sourceNames**: logprobs, logProb, LogProbabilities, TokenLogProbabilities, EnableLogProbabilities
- **term**: Top-K alternatives | **definition**: How many alternative tokens the model is asked to report per generated position. User-settable 1-20, default 5. | **sourceNames**: top_logprobs, LogProbabilitiesTopK, /set logtopk, /logprobs top
- **term**: Derived probability | **definition**: The linear probability of a token, computed as e^(natural-log probability); computed on read, never stored or persisted. | **sourceNames**: TokenLogProbabilities.Probability
- **term**: Fabricated (simulated) confidence data | **definition**: Invented per-token confidence records the hosted backend substitutes when the service returns none, at a uniform 90 percent confidence, returned indistinguishably from measured data. | **sourceNames**: GenerateSimulatedLogProbabilities, simulated logprobs
- **term**: Provider response | **definition**: The object a backend returns from a send: reply text, optional ordered confidence list, elapsed-time value, and error-message slot. | **sourceNames**: AIResponse
- **term**: Token confidence record | **definition**: One entry in the confidence list: token text, natural-log probability, derived probability, and a nullable list of alternatives of the same shape. | **sourceNames**: TokenLogProbabilities
- **term**: Model identifier / deployment name | **definition**: A single settings value that names both the model and the service-side deployment; it is interpolated directly into the request address. | **sourceNames**: ModelId, modelId
- **term**: Library-mediated path | **definition**: The request path taken when token log probabilities are off: a vendor chat client sends the turn with only a temperature option. | **sourceNames**: path P1, GetChatClient, ChatCompletionOptions
- **term**: Direct request path | **definition**: The request path taken when token log probabilities are on: a hand-composed HTTPS request carrying temperature, maximum tokens, nucleus-sampling value, and log-probability options. | **sourceNames**: path P2, CallAzureOpenAIDirectAsync
- **term**: Strict extraction | **definition**: Confidence-data extraction that requires both a token string and a numeric log-probability per entry and accepts alternatives only under the 'top_logprobs' field. | **sourceNames**: ExtractTokenLogProbabilities
- **term**: Tolerant extraction | **definition**: Confidence-data extraction that additionally accepts an alternatives field named 'top_alternatives' and property-map-shaped alternative entries. | **sourceNames**: ExtractTokenLogProbabilitiesFromArray
- **term**: Is-command flag | **definition**: A per-message marker meaning the message was a slash-command echo; such messages are excluded from every upstream request. | **sourceNames**: ChatMessage.IsCommand
- **term**: System-prompt content | **definition**: The resolved text of the currently selected system prompt, held in memory only and always placed at index 0 of every outbound message list. | **sourceNames**: ChatSettings.SystemPromptContent
- **term**: Credential source label | **definition**: The human-readable string naming which leg of the key-resolution chain supplied the API key. | **sourceNames**: ChatSettings.GetCredentialSource
- **term**: Client factory seam | **definition**: The single-method substitutable component that turns (endpoint, key, model identifier) into a chat client, existing so the backend can be tested without network access. | **sourceNames**: IAzureOpenAIClientFactory, DefaultAzureOpenAIClientFactory
- **term**: Display name | **definition**: The human-readable backend name embedded verbatim in user-facing warnings; for the hosted OpenAI backend it is the literal 'Azure OpenAI'. | **sourceNames**: IAIService.GetProviderName()
- **term**: Command prefix | **definition**: The single leading forward-slash character that marks a submitted line as a control instruction rather than a conversational turn. | **sourceNames**: "/" literal tested in ChatShell.ProcessInputAsync / ChatWindow.ProcessInput (StartsWith("/"))
- **term**: Command | **definition**: A named, locally-executed capability exposing a name, a one-sentence description, a usage string and an asynchronous execute operation. | **sourceNames**: ICommand (Name, Description, Usage, ExecuteAsync)
- **term**: Argument vector | **definition**: The ordered list of space-separated text tokens following the command name, excluding the name itself; empty when no arguments were typed. | **sourceNames**: string[] args parameter of ExecuteAsync; parts[1..] in ProcessCommandAsync
- **term**: Command result | **definition**: The uniform three-signal outcome every command returns: whether it worked, an optional message for the user, and whether the session should stop. | **sourceNames**: CommandResult (Success, Message, ExitRequested)
- **term**: Exit request | **definition**: The result flag that tells the host to terminate the session; it is a successful outcome, not an error, and it suppresses rendering of any message on the same result. | **sourceNames**: CommandResult.ExitRequested; CommandResult.ExitResult()
- **term**: Command registry | **definition**: The name-to-command map each host builds once at start-up; the only discovery mechanism, keyed by each command's own declared lower-case name with exact matching. | **sourceNames**: Dictionary<string, ICommand> _commands; InitializeCommands()
- **term**: General help document | **definition**: The curated, hand-authored, topic-grouped listing produced by the help command with no arguments. | **sourceNames**: HelpCommand.GetGeneralHelp()
- **term**: Detailed help | **definition**: The three-line Command/Description/Usage block produced by the help command for one named capability. | **sourceNames**: HelpCommand.ExecuteAsync with args.Length > 0
- **term**: Command listing dialog | **definition**: The full-screen shell's second, independent help surface: an alphabetical name-and-description list opened by F1 or the View Commands menu item. | **sourceNames**: ChatWindow.ShowCommandsDialog / ShowHelp
- **term**: Transient status message | **definition**: A short-lived line of feedback shown in the full-screen shell's status label for 3000 ms before reverting to the standard status text. | **sourceNames**: ChatWindow.ShowStatus / SetStatusMessage with the 3000 ms timer
- **term**: Success marker / failure marker | **definition**: The two Unicode glyphs (U+2713 followed by a space, U+2717 followed by a space) that prefix a rendered command message in the console shell and, for success, in the status line. | **sourceNames**: "✓ " and "✗ " literals in ChatShell/ChatWindow
- **term**: Direct invocation | **definition**: A host path that calls a command by name with a pre-built argument vector, bypassing text parsing and taking on result rendering itself. | **sourceNames**: ChatWindow inject-dialog and change-model-dialog handlers calling ExecuteAsync directly
- **term**: Synthesised command line | **definition**: A host path that builds a slash-prefixed text line from a menu action and re-enters the normal parse-and-dispatch pipeline. | **sourceNames**: ChatWindow.ExecuteCommandAsync("/" + name + args)
- **term**: Line-oriented console shell | **definition**: The plain read-print-loop host that reads a line, dispatches, and prints results with glyph prefixes. | **sourceNames**: src/ChatDbg/ChatShell.cs (assembly Xcaciv.ChatDbg.Shell)
- **term**: Full-screen terminal shell | **definition**: The windowed terminal host with menus, a status bar, modal dialogs and a transcript view that dispatches the same commands. | **sourceNames**: src/ChatDbg.Shell.Gui (Program.cs, UI/ChatWindow.cs)
- **term**: Rendering collaborator | **definition**: The optional formatter a command may be given to draw rich output (marked-up lines, rules, token grids and tables) without the core depending on a UI toolkit; a command must also work without one. | **sourceNames**: IConsoleFormatter; BasicConsoleFormatter; SpectreConsoleFormatter
- **term**: Token log probabilities | **definition**: Per-token likelihood data produced by providers and configured through commands registered in this registry. | **sourceNames**: logprobs / demologprobs commands; EnableLogProbabilities; LogProbabilitiesTopK
- **term**: Conversation transcript | **definition**: The shared, mutable record of messages that transcript commands mutate in place. | **sourceNames**: ChatHistory object shared with Inject/Pop/Clear/Import/Export commands
- **term**: Configuration object | **definition**: The shared, mutable settings record that configuration-bearing commands mutate in place. | **sourceNames**: ChatSettings shared with Set/Model/LogProbs/Prompt/Demo/Tokenize/Inspect commands
- **term**: Managed cloud model marketplace | **definition**: The regional, account-scoped cloud service through which a customer's own cloud account rents access to hosted foundation models. One of the product's three interchangeable chat back ends. | **sourceNames**: Amazon Bedrock, BedrockService, bedrock (registry key), AWSSDK.BedrockRuntime, AmazonBedrockRuntimeClient
- **term**: Back end display name | **definition**: The human-readable name a chat back end reports for itself, interpolated into every warning and error that names it. For this feature it is exactly 'Amazon Bedrock'. | **sourceNames**: IAIService.ProviderName / BedrockService.ProviderName
- **term**: Readiness check | **definition**: The yes/no question 'is this back end configured?', answered from the settings record with no side effects the caller must undo. | **sourceNames**: IsConfigured(ChatSettings)
- **term**: Client-construction seam | **definition**: A substitutable single-operation abstraction that, given a settings record, returns a marketplace client. Exists so the whole build/invoke/parse path can be exercised with zero network. | **sourceNames**: IBedrockRuntimeClientFactory, DefaultBedrockRuntimeClientFactory, CreateClient
- **term**: Model identifier | **definition**: The free-text name of the hosted model to invoke. Its 'anthropic.' prefix (case-insensitive) is the sole model-family discriminator. | **sourceNames**: ModelId, modelId, /set modelId, /model
- **term**: Model family discriminator | **definition**: The literal prefix 'anthropic.' tested case-insensitively against the model identifier to choose between the two request-body shapes. | **sourceNames**: ModelId.StartsWith("anthropic.", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
- **term**: Claude-family request shape | **definition**: The request body carrying anthropic_version, max_tokens, temperature, a top-level system member, messages, logprobs and top_logprobs. | **sourceNames**: the isClaudeModel branch of BedrockService.SendMessageWithLogProbsAsync
- **term**: Generic request shape | **definition**: The stub request body sent to every non-Claude identifier: max_tokens, temperature, a messages array whose index 0 is a synthetic system entry, logprobs and top_logprobs. | **sourceNames**: the else branch of BedrockService.SendMessageWithLogProbsAsync
- **term**: Contract version constant | **definition**: The fixed, never-configurable string 'bedrock-2023-05-31' identifying the Claude-on-marketplace request contract version. | **sourceNames**: anthropic_version
- **term**: Token log probabilities | **definition**: Per-token confidence data: an ordered list of tokens, each with a stored log-probability value and optional ordered alternatives. | **sourceNames**: logprobs, top_logprobs, TokenLogProbabilities, LogProb, TopAlternatives
- **term**: Top-K alternatives | **definition**: The number of alternative tokens requested per position (1-20, default 5). Shapes the request only; never truncates the response. | **sourceNames**: LogProbabilitiesTopK, logProbabilitiesTopK, top_logprobs, topK parameter
- **term**: Probabilities-enabled flag | **definition**: The boolean setting that turns token log probability requesting and parsing on; default off. Both request members are emitted regardless. | **sourceNames**: EnableLogProbabilities, enableLogProbabilities, logprobs
- **term**: Tolerant probability parser | **definition**: The never-throwing response-side probability reader used only from the completion branch; swallows every failure and yields 'no probabilities'. | **sourceNames**: ParseLogProbabilities(JsonElement, int)
- **term**: Structured-content response branch | **definition**: The first response shape: a top-level 'content' member that is a non-empty array; text taken from content[0].text. Has no local tolerance for malformed probability data. | **sourceNames**: content, content[0].text
- **term**: Completion response branch | **definition**: The second response shape: a top-level 'completion' string. | **sourceNames**: completion
- **term**: Unrecognised response outcome | **definition**: The third response shape: the literal answer text 'Unable to parse model response', returned as a normal success. | **sourceNames**: the final else of the response parser
- **term**: Response envelope | **definition**: The shared result object a back end returns: answer text, optional token-probability list, total elapsed time, optional error message. | **sourceNames**: AIResponse, Text, LogProbabilities, TotalTime, ErrorMessage
- **term**: Conversation history | **definition**: The ordered list of turns, each with a role, content text, timestamp, an is-command flag and an optional probability list. | **sourceNames**: ChatHistory, ChatMessage, IsCommand
- **term**: Settings record | **definition**: The in-memory configuration object read (never written) by this back end, carrying provider, model identifier, region, tuning values, probability settings and computed secret values. | **sourceNames**: ChatSettings
- **term**: Settings document | **definition**: The persisted configuration file at <user profile>/.ChatDbg/settings.json, indented with camel-cased member names, falling back to the system temporary directory. | **sourceNames**: settings.json, SettingsService
- **term**: Credential source label | **definition**: The user-visible description of where a resolved credential came from: 'environment variable (<NAME>)', 'Windows Credential Manager', 'settings file (deprecated)', or 'not set'. | **sourceNames**: GetCredentialSource
- **term**: Operating-system credential vault | **definition**: An optional, capability-detected secret store used as the third credential source; degrades silently to 'not found' where unavailable. | **sourceNames**: WindowsCredentialManager, advapi32.dll, CredReadW/CredWriteW/CredDeleteW/CredFree, ChatDbg:AwsAccessKey, ChatDbg:AwsSecretKey
- **term**: Default cloud credential discovery chain | **definition**: The vendor SDK's own credential search (shared profile file, container/instance role, single sign-on, environment variables), used only as the fallback when either resolved key is empty. | **sourceNames**: AmazonBedrockRuntimeClient(RegionEndpoint) constructor
- **term**: Diagnostic trace channel | **definition**: The developer-build-only sink that records the full request body, the full reply body, failure detail and probability-parse failures. | **sourceNames**: System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine, "Bedrock request:", "Bedrock response:", "Error in BedrockService:", "Error parsing log probabilities:"
- **term**: Release/teardown operation | **definition**: The back end's advertised disposal step, which does nothing but set an internal already-released flag and is idempotent. | **sourceNames**: IDisposable.Dispose, _disposed, finalizer
- **term**: Credential resolution | **definition**: The per-read procedure that walks the three credential channels in fixed priority (environment variables, then OS credential vault, then deprecated settings-file field) and returns the first non-empty value, coalescing 'no value' to the empty string. | **sourceNames**: ChatSettings.GetSecureValue
- **term**: Credential source label | **definition**: A short, non-disclosing string naming which channel supplied a credential; one of 'environment variable (<VARNAME>)', 'Windows Credential Manager', 'settings file (deprecated)', or 'not set'. | **sourceNames**: ChatSettings.GetCredentialSource
- **term**: OS credential vault | **definition**: The operating-system-managed, encrypted-at-rest, per-user secret store used as credential tier 2; available on Windows only in the source. | **sourceNames**: WindowsCredentialManager, Windows Credential Manager, advapi32 CredReadW/CredWriteW/CredDeleteW
- **term**: Vault-enabled flag | **definition**: The single persisted boolean, default false, that makes the OS credential vault tier eligible during resolution; may only be set true on a platform that has a vault (except via the graphical dialog, which does not check). | **sourceNames**: useWindowsCredentialManager, ChatSettings.UseWindowsCredentialManager
- **term**: Vault entry name | **definition**: The fixed, colon-containing opaque key under which each secret is stored in the OS vault: ChatDbg:AzureApiKey, ChatDbg:AwsAccessKey, ChatDbg:AwsSecretKey. | **sourceNames**: target name, credential target, ChatDbg:* literals
- **term**: Deprecated plaintext credential field | **definition**: One of the three settings-file string fields that hold a secret in the clear; still serialized and still read as tier 3, but no product code path ever writes a non-empty value into them. | **sourceNames**: azureApiKey, awsAccessKey, awsSecretKey (ChatSettings)
- **term**: Plaintext-credentials-present | **definition**: The derived condition, true when at least one of the three deprecated settings-file credential fields is non-empty, that drives the standing load-time warning and gates the migration wizard. | **sourceNames**: ChatSettings.HasPlaintextCredentials
- **term**: Migration wizard | **definition**: The interactive flow that shows a platform-conditional three-option menu, prints migration instructions or writes secrets into the vault, and then offers to erase the plaintext copies from the settings file. | **sourceNames**: SettingsService.MigrateCredentials, /set migrate
- **term**: Masked status token | **definition**: The literal '***set***' printed in the status display in place of a present secret, paired with '(not set)' for an absent one. | **sourceNames**: SetCommand credential status formatter
- **term**: Store-a-secret command | **definition**: The command that places a named credential into the OS vault; requires at least three tokens, requires the vault-enabled flag to already be true, and accepts short type aliases. | **sourceNames**: /set wincred, SettingsService.StoreCredentialSecurely
- **term**: Enable-vault consent flow | **definition**: The interactive y/N prompt that must be answered 'y' or 'yes' before the OS vault tier is turned on and persisted. | **sourceNames**: /set enablewincred, SettingsService.EnableWindowsCredentialManager
- **term**: Legacy credential-key refusal | **definition**: The always-failing response to any attempt to set a secret through the ordinary settings-change command, carrying per-credential remediation text. | **sourceNames**: SetCommand credential-key guard, GetCredentialSecurityMessage
- **term**: Settings store | **definition**: The component that resolves the settings file path, loads and saves the settings record, and emits the credential warnings during load. | **sourceNames**: SettingsService, ISettingsService
- **term**: Settings record | **definition**: The single in-memory configuration object that carries the vault-enabled flag, the three deprecated plaintext fields, and the three computed resolved credential values. | **sourceNames**: ChatSettings
- **term**: Local-machine persistence scope | **definition**: The vault persistence setting (value 2) that keeps an entry on one machine across logoff and reboot and explicitly does not roam with a domain profile. | **sourceNames**: CRED_PERSIST_LOCAL_MACHINE, persistence = 2
- **term**: Generic credential kind | **definition**: The vault entry kind (value 1) used for every read, write and delete performed by this product; the vault's six other kinds are declared but never selected. | **sourceNames**: CRED_TYPE_GENERIC, credential type 1
- **term**: Ambient credential chain fallback | **definition**: The undocumented fourth credential channel a consumer inherits when the cloud-inference client is constructed without both halves of the key pair and falls back to the vendor SDK's own credential discovery. | **sourceNames**: DefaultBedrockRuntimeClientFactory default client construction
- **term**: Conversation record | **definition**: The single in-memory, ordered list of turns kept for the life of one running process, plus its session identifier and created-at timestamp. It is what the model provider receives on every request. | **sourceNames**: ChatHistory, chat history, history
- **term**: Message (turn) | **definition**: One element of the conversation record: a role, content text, a UTC timestamp, a hidden-from-model flag, and optional token log probabilities. | **sourceNames**: ChatMessage, message
- **term**: Role | **definition**: The speaker label on a message. Inject enforces exactly three lower-cased values (user, assistant, system); append and import enforce nothing. | **sourceNames**: Role, role
- **term**: Hidden-from-model flag | **definition**: A per-message boolean that, when set, excludes the message from every hosted-provider outbound request while leaving it in the record and in the transcript. Nothing in the shipping product ever sets it. | **sourceNames**: IsCommand, isCommand
- **term**: Token log probabilities | **definition**: The per-token probability payload attached to a model reply, stored on the message and persisted through export/import. Owned by the token-probability feature; this feature only stores and restores it. | **sourceNames**: LogProbabilities, logProbabilities, logprobs, logprob
- **term**: Alternate token candidates | **definition**: The nested list of other tokens the model considered at a given position, embedded inside each token log-probability entry. | **sourceNames**: TopAlternatives, top_alternatives
- **term**: Has token log probabilities | **definition**: A derived, never-persisted predicate that is true only when a message's token log-probability list exists AND has at least one element. Drives the transcript marker and the analysis panel. | **sourceNames**: HasLogProbabilities
- **term**: Session identifier | **definition**: A random unique identifier generated when the process starts, persisted on export and overwritten on import. Never read by anything else in the product. | **sourceNames**: SessionId, sessionId
- **term**: Created-at timestamp | **definition**: The UTC instant at which the conversation record was constructed. Written on export, read but discarded on import, never mutated, never reset by clear. | **sourceNames**: CreatedAt, createdAt
- **term**: Inject | **definition**: The operation that fabricates a message with a chosen role and inserts it at a chosen index in the conversation record. | **sourceNames**: InjectCommand, /inject
- **term**: Pop | **definition**: The operation that removes exactly one message — the last — from the conversation record. | **sourceNames**: PopCommand, /pop
- **term**: Clear | **definition**: The operation that empties the message list while preserving the session identifier and created-at timestamp. | **sourceNames**: ClearCommand, /clear
- **term**: Export | **definition**: The operation that writes the whole conversation record to a user-named file in the conversation-file format. | **sourceNames**: ExportCommand, /export, SaveHistoryAsync
- **term**: Import | **definition**: The operation that replaces the live conversation record's messages and session identifier wholesale from a file. It replaces, never merges. | **sourceNames**: ImportCommand, /import, LoadHistoryAsync
- **term**: Conversation history file | **definition**: The exported on-disk artifact: a single JSON object holding messages, session identifier and created-at timestamp. It is a wire format — an exported file must remain re-importable. | **sourceNames**: chat_history.json, chat.json
- **term**: Token-probability marker | **definition**: The clickable diamond glyph drawn beneath an assistant turn in the full-screen transcript when that turn carries token log probabilities. | **sourceNames**: the diamond marker drawn by ChatWindow
- **term**: Plain-console shell | **definition**: The line-oriented read-eval-print shell that prints each turn as it happens and maintains no rendered view. | **sourceNames**: src/ChatDbg/ChatShell.cs, Xcaciv.ChatDbg.Shell
- **term**: Full-screen terminal shell | **definition**: The windowed terminal shell with menus, dialogs, a redrawn transcript and a status bar. | **sourceNames**: src/ChatDbg.Shell.Gui, ChatWindow, Terminal.Gui shell
- **term**: Settings record | **definition**: The single shared, mutable, process-wide tunables record holding provider selection, model addressing, generation parameters, token-display options, local-model performance knobs, the OS-keystore toggle and the deprecated in-document secret slots. | **sourceNames**: ChatSettings
- **term**: Settings document | **definition**: The indented, hand-editable structured text file at <user profile>/.ChatDbg/settings.json that persists the settings record in full on every mutation. | **sourceNames**: settings.json, SettingsService.GetSettingsFilePath, _settingsFilePath
- **term**: Settings store / persistence port | **definition**: The narrow six-operation contract through which everything outside the feature reaches persistence (persist, load, report path, store one credential, interactive keystore-enable, interactive migration). | **sourceNames**: ISettingsService, SettingsService
- **term**: Token log probabilities | **definition**: Per-token probability data the product can request from a provider and render; five settings-record fields control whether it is requested and how it is displayed. | **sourceNames**: logprobs, EnableLogProbabilities, LogProbsCommand
- **term**: Top-K alternatives | **definition**: How many alternative tokens are requested and shown per position; inclusive range 1..20, default 5. | **sourceNames**: LogProbabilitiesTopK, logtopk, /logprobs top
- **term**: Grid-view maximum alternatives | **definition**: Cap on alternatives rendered per token in the grid layout; inclusive range 1..20, default 5. | **sourceNames**: GridViewMaxAlternatives, gridmaxalt
- **term**: Show-all-tokens mode | **definition**: Display flag choosing between rendering every token and rendering only sampled tokens (beginning, middle, end). | **sourceNames**: ShowAllTokens, /logprobs showall, /logprobs showsample
- **term**: Grid layout / list layout | **definition**: The two token-display layouts selectable for token log probability rendering. | **sourceNames**: GridViewForTokens, tokensgrid, /logprobs grid, /logprobs list
- **term**: Local model provider | **definition**: The in-process provider that loads a local model file from disk; stored provider value is the literal 'llama'. | **sourceNames**: llama, LLamaSharpService
- **term**: Local-model tunables | **definition**: Context size, GPU layer count, GPU device(s), thread count and batch size; only the first two reach the runtime. | **sourceNames**: llamaContextSize, llamaGpuLayerCount, llamaGpuDevice, llamaThreads, llamaBatchSize
- **term**: OS credential keystore toggle | **definition**: The persisted boolean that enables tier-2 credential lookup in the operating system's native secret store. | **sourceNames**: useWindowsCredentialManager, wincred, enablewincred, UseWindowsCredentialManager
- **term**: Deprecated in-document secret slots | **definition**: Three legacy fields (Azure key, AWS access key, AWS secret key) that are always written to the document as empty strings, cannot be set via commands, and are cleared only by the migration flow. | **sourceNames**: azureApiKey, awsAccessKey, awsSecretKey
- **term**: Resolved credential | **definition**: A secret value computed on every read from the three-tier order (environment variable, then keystore when the toggle is on, then the deprecated in-document slot); never persisted. | **sourceNames**: GetSecureValue, AzureApiKeySecure, AwsAccessKeySecure, AwsSecretKeySecure
- **term**: Credential source string | **definition**: A one-of-four report of where a resolved credential came from: environment variable (NAME), Windows Credential Manager, settings file (deprecated), not set. | **sourceNames**: GetCredentialSource
- **term**: System-prompt name / system-prompt body | **definition**: The prompt is referenced by a persisted name; its body is never persisted and is re-resolved at every startup, falling back to a built-in default body. | **sourceNames**: SystemPromptName, SystemPrompt
- **term**: Configuration self-check | **definition**: The startup routine that reports whether the selected provider is empty, unknown, recognised-but-unconfigured, or configured, with provider-specific remediation text. | **sourceNames**: ChatShell startup validation block
- **term**: Provider is-configured predicate | **definition**: Each provider's read-only test over the settings record deciding whether it can serve a chat turn. | **sourceNames**: IsConfigured
- **term**: Clamp-vs-reject policy | **definition**: The observed split whereby the text commands reject out-of-range numbers while the windowed dialog silently clamps them to the nearest bound. | **sourceNames**: SetCommand validation vs SettingsDialog Math.Clamp calls
- **term**: Delegated setting key | **definition**: A /set sub-key (wincred, enablewincred, migrate) that hands off to Credential Management and deliberately skips the trailing save because the delegate already persisted. | **sourceNames**: wincred, enablewincred, migrate
- **term**: Save-after-every-change guarantee | **definition**: Every successful mutation writes the entire record to disk immediately; there is no save verb and no dirty tracking. | **sourceNames**: SaveSettingsAsync call sites
- **term**: System prompt | **definition**: A named block of instruction text that sets the assistant's persona and focus; injected as the conversation's system message on every model call. | **sourceNames**: SystemPrompt, systemPrompt, ChatSettings.SystemPromptContent
- **term**: Prompt record | **definition**: One persisted system prompt: name, content, description, created-at and last-used-at. One record per file; the record is the entire file. | **sourceNames**: SystemPrompt (Models/SystemPrompt.cs)
- **term**: Prompt library / prompt store | **definition**: The directory of prompt record files plus the capabilities for fetching, listing, saving, deleting, stamping-as-used, and reporting its own directory. | **sourceNames**: SystemPromptService, ISystemPromptService, the system_prompts directory
- **term**: Prompt text / prompt content | **definition**: The literal instruction string handed to the model. Multi-line, unbounded in length. | **sourceNames**: SystemPrompt.Content, content JSON key, ChatSettings.SystemPromptContent
- **term**: Active prompt | **definition**: The single prompt whose text is injected into every model call. Identified by name only; the name is persisted, the text is runtime-only and re-derived at every launch. | **sourceNames**: ChatSettings.SystemPromptName (persisted, default "default") plus ChatSettings.SystemPromptContent (JsonIgnore)
- **term**: Built-in prompts / seed set | **definition**: The four prompts written automatically whenever the library loads zero records: default, code-reviewer, algorithm-helper, security-expert. | **sourceNames**: CreateDefaultPrompts (SystemPromptService.cs:157-204)
- **term**: Last-used timestamp | **definition**: The UTC instant a prompt was most recently selected as active; absent until first use and rendered as the literal 'Never used'. | **sourceNames**: SystemPrompt.LastUsedAt, lastUsedAt JSON key, UpdateLastUsedAsync
- **term**: Created time | **definition**: The UTC instant the record was constructed; never updated after creation, reset by re-import. | **sourceNames**: SystemPrompt.CreatedAt, createdAt JSON key
- **term**: Prompt description | **definition**: Short display-only text shown in listings and detail views; changeable only through the graphical edit form. | **sourceNames**: SystemPrompt.Description, description JSON key
- **term**: Sanitized file name | **definition**: The record's name with every host-OS-forbidden filename character replaced by an underscore, plus a .json extension. Lossy, so distinct names can collide onto one file. | **sourceNames**: SanitizeFileName / Path.GetInvalidFileNameChars (SystemPromptService.cs:209-214)
- **term**: Content-entry mode | **definition**: The console shell's interactive multi-line editor state, entered by /prompt edit and left only by typing the sentinel line END. | **sourceNames**: PromptCommand edit read loop (:258-285), the 'END' sentinel
- **term**: Current marker | **definition**: The literal suffix ' (current)' appended in listings to the entry whose name equals the active-prompt name. | **sourceNames**: PromptCommand.cs:91
- **term**: Graphical prompt manager | **definition**: The modal 80x25 management screen in the full-screen terminal shell, reached from Tools -> System Prompts... | **sourceNames**: SystemPromptsDialog (UI/SystemPromptsDialog.cs)
- **term**: Name placeholder <n> | **definition**: The literal placeholder token used in every usage and error string where a prompt NAME is meant (not a number); consistent across code, error messages and the user manual. | **sourceNames**: '<n>' in PromptCommand.cs:25,114,150,178,218,246,291,333
- **term**: Prompt record file | **definition**: The on-disk wire format of one prompt: a flat five-key object with keys name, content, description, createdAt, lastUsedAt, matched case-sensitively, with lastUsedAt written as explicit null when unset. | **sourceNames**: JsonPropertyName attributes on SystemPrompt.cs:5-17
- **term**: Diagnostic recorder | **definition**: The in-process object that owns the capture buffer, the output switches and the log directory, and performs arming, recording, flushing, snapshot export and shutdown. Exactly one exists per local-inference subsystem instance. | **sourceNames**: LLamaSharpLogConfig
- **term**: Capture buffer (log buffer) | **definition**: The single in-memory text accumulator holding whole formatted entry lines separated by platform line terminators; drained by flush, emptied by clear, snapshotted non-destructively by export. | **sourceNames**: _logBuffer, GetLogs()
- **term**: Arm capture | **definition**: The one-shot, idempotent operation that ensures the log directory exists, installs the recorder as the inference engine's process-global diagnostic destination, sets the armed latch and records its own confirmation entry. | **sourceNames**: ConfigureLogging()
- **term**: Engine diagnostic line | **definition**: A diagnostic message emitted by the native inference engine about itself, delivered to the recorder with an opaque severity token and a message string; trailing whitespace is stripped before buffering. | **sourceNames**: NativeLogConfig.llama_log_set callback, LLamaLogLevel
- **term**: Application entry | **definition**: A commentary line recorded by the application about what it just asked the engine to do, carrying a free-text severity label and a message; not whitespace-stripped and never triggers the size-based auto-flush. | **sourceNames**: LogMessage(level, message)
- **term**: Flush (append-and-drain) | **definition**: Appending the whole capture buffer to today's rolling daily file and then emptying the buffer. A no-op that does not drain when file capture is disabled or the buffer is empty. | **sourceNames**: FlushLogs()
- **term**: Forced flush | **definition**: A deliberate flush invoked at one of exactly five hazard points (request-level error, end of each of the two generation paths, model-load failure, context-creation failure) so evidence is on disk before a native crash can destroy it. | **sourceNames**: _logConfig.FlushLogs() call sites in LLamaSharpService
- **term**: Snapshot export | **definition**: Overwriting a caller-named file with the current capture buffer content, without clearing the buffer; produces a 0-byte file when the buffer is empty and reports nothing to the caller. | **sourceNames**: SaveLogsToFile(path), LLamaSharpService.SaveSystemLogs
- **term**: Rolling daily log file | **definition**: A plain-text, append-only, UTF-8 (no byte-order mark) file named llamasharp_<yyyyMMdd>.log inside the log directory, created lazily on the first non-empty flush of a local calendar day and never pruned, capped or compressed. | **sourceNames**: llamasharp_{DateTime.Now:yyyyMMdd}.log
- **term**: Log directory | **definition**: The folder holding rolling daily files; defaults to the per-user roaming application-data root joined with ChatDbg then Logs. Created only during arming and only when file capture is enabled at that moment. | **sourceNames**: LogDirectory
- **term**: File capture | **definition**: The switch, on by default, that gates both flushing and the size-triggered auto-flush. | **sourceNames**: EnableFileLogging
- **term**: Debug-stream echo | **definition**: The switch, on by default, that mirrors each formatted entry to the platform's attachable debug/trace output stream; also the only channel on which this feature reports its own internal failures. | **sourceNames**: EnableDebugOutput, System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine
- **term**: Console echo | **definition**: The switch, off by default, that mirrors each formatted entry to standard output, interleaving log lines into the user's chat transcript. | **sourceNames**: EnableConsoleOutput
- **term**: Maximum buffer size | **definition**: The character-count threshold, default 10000, above which an engine-sourced append immediately triggers a flush. Unvalidated: any integer including zero and negatives is accepted. | **sourceNames**: MaxBufferSize
- **term**: Armed latch | **definition**: A private one-way false-to-true flag marking that engine capture has been successfully installed; never reset, not even by shutdown. | **sourceNames**: _isConfigured
- **term**: Export-logs command | **definition**: The user-typed command intended to write captured diagnostics to a named file; at the source commit a stub that validates argument count only, writes nothing, and is registered with neither shell. | **sourceNames**: ExportLogsCommand, /export-logs
- **term**: Token log probabilities / token-probability capture | **definition**: The generation mode that records per-token probability data; when active it is the only mode that emits per-token DEBUG entries into the diagnostic trail. | **sourceNames**: logprobs, sampling pipeline probability capture
- **term**: Interactive chat session | **definition**: The line-oriented terminal session that owns the terminal for the life of the process: prints a prompt, reads one line, decides command-vs-conversation, drives the provider, renders the reply, and shuts down. | **sourceNames**: ChatShell (src/ChatDbg/ChatShell.cs), "console shell", REPL
- **term**: Conversation turn | **definition**: A typed line that does not begin with a forward slash and is therefore sent to the language-model provider as a user message. | **sourceNames**: chat turn, SendChatMessageAsync, ProcessChatAsync
- **term**: Control instruction | **definition**: A typed line beginning with a forward slash, handled locally by a registered command rather than sent to the model. | **sourceNames**: command, ICommand, ProcessCommandAsync
- **term**: Token log probabilities / per-token probabilities | **definition**: The ordered list of per-token confidence values plus their most-likely alternatives that a provider can return alongside a reply. | **sourceNames**: logprobs, logProbabilities, TokenLogProbabilities, LogProb
- **term**: Token probability analysis | **definition**: The product capability that requests, stores and visualizes per-token probabilities for a reply. | **sourceNames**: enableLogProbabilities, /logprobs, LogProbsCommand
- **term**: Probabilities top-K | **definition**: How many alternative tokens the provider is asked to return for each generated token. Default 5, valid range 1-20. | **sourceNames**: logProbabilitiesTopK, top-k, /logprobs top
- **term**: Sample mode / show-all mode | **definition**: The display switch that decides whether the whole token list is rendered or only three five-token blocks from the beginning, middle and end. | **sourceNames**: showAllTokens, /logprobs showall, /logprobs showsample
- **term**: Grid layout / list layout | **definition**: The two token-visualization layouts: a grid of bordered cards, or a four-column bordered table. | **sourceNames**: gridViewForTokens, /logprobs grid, /logprobs list
- **term**: Grid alternative cap | **definition**: The maximum alternatives shown on one grid card before a '+ N more' summary. Default 5, valid range 1-20. Does not apply to list layout, which is fixed at 3. | **sourceNames**: gridViewMaxAlternatives, gridmaxalt
- **term**: Live settings record | **definition**: The single mutable configuration object shared by reference with every command and provider adapter, so a mutation is visible to the loop immediately. | **sourceNames**: ChatSettings, _settings
- **term**: Transcript | **definition**: The in-memory ordered conversation, never auto-persisted, discarded at exit unless explicitly exported. | **sourceNames**: ChatHistory, chat history, messages
- **term**: Provider adapter | **definition**: One of three interchangeable back ends addressed by the lowercase keys azure, bedrock, llama, each offering a display name, a network-free configuration self-check, a plain send, a send-with-probabilities, and disposal. | **sourceNames**: IAIService, AzureOpenAIService, BedrockService, LLamaSharpService, _aiServices
- **term**: Command result | **definition**: The uniform three-signal outcome every control instruction returns: success, an optional human-readable message, and an exit request. | **sourceNames**: CommandResult, Success, Message, ExitRequested
- **term**: Credential source label | **definition**: A human-readable string naming where a credential was resolved from, printed instead of the credential value itself. | **sourceNames**: GetCredentialSource, AzureApiKeySource, AwsAccessKeySource
- **term**: Machine secret vault | **definition**: The optional, platform-specific operating-system credential store used as credential priority level 2 when explicitly enabled. | **sourceNames**: Windows Credential Manager, WindowsCredentialManager, useWindowsCredentialManager, wincred
- **term**: System prompt | **definition**: The named instruction text prepended to every model request, loaded at startup from a per-name file and stamped with a last-used timestamp on each start. | **sourceNames**: SystemPrompt, systemPromptName, systemPromptContent
- **term**: Reply envelope | **definition**: The structure a provider returns carrying reply text, an optional probability list, an error message and a total elapsed time; this session reads only the first two. | **sourceNames**: AIResponse, Text, LogProbabilities, ErrorMessage, TotalTime
- **term**: Local-model file | **definition**: The on-disk model file the local inference provider loads; its path doubles as the model identifier and its existence is the provider's entire configuration check. | **sourceNames**: GGUF, modelId, .gguf
- **term**: Startup credential advisory | **definition**: The one-shot, read-only diagnostic printed after the banner reporting whether the active provider is configured and, if so, where its credential came from. | **sourceNames**: CheckServiceConfiguration
- **term**: Token confidence record | **definition**: The per-token unit of analysis: the text of one generated token, the log-probability the model assigned to it, and an optional ordered list of alternative tokens the model weighed at that position. Recursive — alternatives are records of the same shape. | **sourceNames**: TokenLogProbability, TokenLogProbabilities.cs, logprobs entry, logProbabilities list member
- **term**: Token log-probability (stored value) | **definition**: The natural-log probability the model assigned to a token, stored verbatim with no range validation. | **sourceNames**: LogProb, logprob, log_prob
- **term**: Derived probability | **definition**: A computed, never-persisted value equal to e raised to the stored log-probability; recomputed on every read. | **sourceNames**: Probability (computed property), Math.Exp(LogProb)
- **term**: Alternative token | **definition**: One of the other tokens the model nearly chose at a given position, carried with its own stored value; nested alternatives are normally absent. | **sourceNames**: TopAlternatives, top_alternatives, top_logprobs
- **term**: Top-K alternatives | **definition**: How many alternative tokens are requested from the provider per generated position. Default 5, valid range 1-20. | **sourceNames**: logProbabilitiesTopK, logtopk, LogProbabilitiesTopK, top_logprobs request field
- **term**: Confidence capture (capture-enabled) | **definition**: The single global switch that decides, per conversation turn, whether the send-with-probabilities operation or the plain send-text operation is used. Default false. | **sourceNames**: enableLogProbabilities, logprobs (set alias), EnableLogProbabilities
- **term**: Show-all-tokens vs sampled display | **definition**: Display mode choosing between rendering every token and rendering a beginning/middle/end sample of 5 tokens each. Default is sampled. | **sourceNames**: showAllTokens, /logprobs showall, /logprobs showsample
- **term**: Grid layout vs list layout | **definition**: Display layout choosing between a responsive grid of per-token cards and a detailed row-per-token table. Default is list layout. | **sourceNames**: gridViewForTokens, tokensgrid, /logprobs grid, /logprobs list
- **term**: Grid max alternatives | **definition**: Display-only cap on how many alternatives are drawn on each grid card before a '+N more' indicator. Default 5, valid range 1-20. | **sourceNames**: gridViewMaxAlternatives, gridmaxalt
- **term**: Sampled token view (beginning/middle/end) | **definition**: The three 5-token segments shown when the token count exceeds 15: indices 0-4, count/2-2 onward, and count-5 onward. | **sourceNames**: SampleTokens, GetSampledTokens, sampleSize = 5
- **term**: Probability configuration command | **definition**: The user-facing command that reports, configures and diagnoses token probability analysis. | **sourceNames**: /logprobs, LogProbsCommand
- **term**: Offline demonstration mode | **definition**: A command that fabricates a sample confidence record from a fixed sentence and renders it without contacting any provider. | **sourceNames**: /demologprobs, DemoLogProbsCommand, SampleData
- **term**: Confidence colour band | **definition**: The mapping from a confidence value to a colour name: >=90 green, >=70 lime, >=50 yellow, >=30 orange, else red. | **sourceNames**: GetColorForProbability, TokenFormatters.cs colour bands
- **term**: Heat map bucket | **definition**: The full-screen shell's ten-bucket colour mapping computed as clamp(truncate(probability x 10), 0, 9) on a 0-1 scale. | **sourceNames**: GetColorForLogProb, ChatWindow heat map, LogProbHeatmapView
- **term**: Token probability side panel | **definition**: The framed, scrollable region in the full-screen shell that lists a chosen message's tokens and alternatives. | **sourceNames**: logProbsPanel, 'Token Probabilities' FrameView, ChatWindow.cs
- **term**: Confidence indicator | **definition**: The clickable diamond glyph appended beneath any assistant message that carries confidence data, which binds the side panel to that message. | **sourceNames**: the '◊' button, logprob indicator button
- **term**: Response record | **definition**: The transport object returned by a provider adapter: response text, optional confidence list, total elapsed seconds, optional error message. | **sourceNames**: AIResponse, AIResponse.cs
- **term**: Has-confidence-data flag | **definition**: A derived, never-persisted boolean on a conversation message: the confidence list is non-null and its count is greater than zero. | **sourceNames**: HasLogProbabilities, ChatMessage.cs:17-18
- **term**: Plain-text confidence report | **definition**: The dependency-free textual report headed '=== Token Probabilities Analysis ===' with a markdown-ish table and a 39-character footer rule. | **sourceNames**: GenerateTokenProbabilityReport, TokenFormatters.cs:62-89
- **term**: Token log probabilities | **definition**: Per-token confidence data returned alongside generated text: the token's own text, the model's log-probability for it, and an ordered list of alternative tokens the model considered. | **sourceNames**: logprobs, LogProbs, TokenLogProbabilities, enableLogProbabilities, logProbabilitiesTopK, /logprobs
- **term**: Token probability record | **definition**: One entry in the token log probability list: token text, log probability, computed probability (e raised to the log probability), and an optional ordered list of alternatives of the same shape. | **sourceNames**: TokenLogProbabilities, TokenLogProbability
- **term**: Output surface contract | **definition**: The six-operation abstraction (write markup line, write plain line, write blank line, write separator, display token grid, display token table) through which in-process features emit user-visible output without knowing which shell hosts them. | **sourceNames**: IConsoleFormatter
- **term**: Plain output surface | **definition**: The dependency-free implementation of the output surface contract that strips styling directives and writes only to a replaceable standard-output writer. | **sourceNames**: BasicConsoleFormatter
- **term**: Styled output surface | **definition**: The implementation of the output surface contract that emits inline square-bracket styling directives to a rich terminal library, producing coloured cards, tables, panels and captioned rules. | **sourceNames**: SpectreConsoleFormatter
- **term**: Shared token-presentation helpers | **definition**: The library of format-only routines shared across surfaces: escape a token for display, map a probability to a colour band name, format a probability as a percentage, build a plain-text token report, build a compact alternatives description. | **sourceNames**: TokenFormatters
- **term**: Confidence colour band map | **definition**: A mapping from a token's probability to a colour, used to make low-confidence regions of an answer visible at a glance. Four mutually inconsistent maps exist in the product. | **sourceNames**: GetColorForProbability, GetProbabilityColor, GetHeatMapColor, probabilityColorSchemes
- **term**: Card grid layout | **definition**: The dense multi-column layout that draws one bordered card per token, chosen for spotting patterns across many tokens. | **sourceNames**: gridViewForTokens, DisplayTokenGrid, grid view
- **term**: Row-per-token table layout | **definition**: The detailed layout that draws one table row per token with its text, probability and top alternatives, chosen for inspecting individual tokens. | **sourceNames**: DisplayTokenTable, list view
- **term**: Beginning/middle/end sampling | **definition**: The default volume-control rule that renders only three slices of a long token list instead of every token. | **sourceNames**: showAllTokens, GetSampleTokens, SampleSize
- **term**: Grid max alternatives | **definition**: The user-configurable cap on how many alternative tokens are listed per token in the card grid and in the full-screen probability panel, before a 'plus N more' summary. | **sourceNames**: gridViewMaxAlternatives, GridViewMaxAlternatives, maxAlternatives
- **term**: Top-K alternatives | **definition**: The user-configurable count of alternative tokens requested from the provider, which bounds how many can ever be drawn. | **sourceNames**: logProbabilitiesTopK, LogProbabilitiesTopK
- **term**: Token probability panel | **definition**: The full-screen interface's dockable side panel that lists every token of one selected reply with its ranked alternatives, colour-coded by confidence. | **sourceNames**: logProbsPanel, ChatWindow probability panel, ShowLogProbsPanel
- **term**: Probability indicator | **definition**: The small focusable lozenge control appended below any reply that carries probability data; activating it opens the probability panel on that reply. | **sourceNames**: ◊ lozenge, lozengeScheme, logProbIndicator
- **term**: Flowing heat-map view | **definition**: A defined-but-never-constructed view that paints tokens as a continuous paragraph with each token's background coloured by its probability. | **sourceNames**: LogProbHeatmapView
- **term**: Transcript | **definition**: The scrollable rendering of the conversation history in the full-screen interface, with per-role colour schemes and alignment. | **sourceNames**: chatView, RefreshChatView, Chat History frame
- **term**: Idle status text | **definition**: The persistent one-line readout above the status bar showing which provider, model and system prompt are in effect; transient messages replace it for three seconds. | **sourceNames**: statusLabel, UpdateStatusLabel, ShowStatusMessage
- **term**: Theme applier | **definition**: The single argument-free operation that installs the fixed dark colour scheme into the interface toolkit's four global scheme slots at start-up. | **sourceNames**: ThemeManager.ApplyDarkTheme
- **term**: Demonstration visualisation | **definition**: A command that renders fabricated sample token probability data so a user can see the visualisation without configuring a provider or spending a request. | **sourceNames**: /demologprobs, DemoLogProbsCommand
- **term**: Escape formatting | **definition**: Replacing a token's control characters with visible two-character escape sequences so an invisible token is not mistaken for a blank. | **sourceNames**: FormatTokenForDisplay, EscapeTokenText
- **term**: Numbering start index | **definition**: A caller-supplied offset added to each rendered token's position so that a slice of a longer list still shows each token's true position in the full response. | **sourceNames**: startIndex
- **term**: Build profile | **definition**: A named set of compilation and packaging switches selected by name at build time. Four exist: development, ordinary optimised, native/smallest, and bundled/single-file. | **sourceNames**: Configuration; Debug; Release; Compact; SingleFile; PropertyGroup Condition="'$(Configuration)'=='Compact'"
- **term**: Native profile | **definition**: The build profile that compiles ahead-of-time to native machine code with whole-framework dead-code removal, size-first code generation, symbol stripping and no identity metadata. Never actually released. | **sourceNames**: Compact configuration; PublishAot
- **term**: Bundled profile | **definition**: The build profile that actually ships: one compressed self-extracting file, just-in-time compiled, conservatively trimmed, retaining identity metadata. | **sourceNames**: SingleFile configuration; PublishSingleFile; EnableCompressionInSingleFile
- **term**: Dead-code elimination | **definition**: Reachability-based removal of unreferenced code from the artefact, with two selectable aggressiveness levels (whole-framework vs opt-in-only). | **sourceNames**: trimming; PublishTrimmed; TrimMode=full; TrimMode=partial; IL trimmer; tree shaking
- **term**: Ahead-of-time native compilation | **definition**: Compiling the managed program to native machine code at build time so no just-in-time compiler ships and start-up is process load only. | **sourceNames**: AOT; PublishAot; Native AOT; IlcOptimizationPreference
- **term**: Self-contained artefact | **definition**: An executable that embeds the language runtime so the target machine needs nothing pre-installed. | **sourceNames**: SelfContained; --self-contained; UseAppHost
- **term**: Platform triple | **definition**: The identifier naming the operating system and processor architecture a build targets, e.g. 64-bit Windows or 64-bit Linux. Appears literally inside published asset names. | **sourceNames**: runtime identifier; RID; win-x64; linux-x64; osx-x64; osx-arm64; -r flag
- **term**: Toolchain pin document | **definition**: The repository-root machine-readable file declaring the exact build toolchain version and its roll-forward policy. | **sourceNames**: global.json; sdk.version; rollForward: latestFeature
- **term**: Target framework identifier | **definition**: The single value all four projects compile against, corresponding to runtime major version 10. | **sourceNames**: TargetFramework; net10.0; TFM; framework moniker
- **term**: Runtime-configuration side-car | **definition**: The companion configuration document a bundled just-in-time artefact needs in order to start. Suppressed in both distribution profiles. | **sourceNames**: GenerateRuntimeConfigurationFiles; runtimeconfig.json; runtimeOptions; configProperties
- **term**: Culture-invariant operation | **definition**: Running with no culture data present, so comparison, casing and formatting collapse to invariant behaviour regardless of machine locale. | **sourceNames**: InvariantGlobalization
- **term**: Symbolic message keys | **definition**: Replacement of framework exception and message sentences with short identifier keys to reduce artefact size; user-visible in error output. | **sourceNames**: UseSystemResourceKeys
- **term**: Self-extraction set | **definition**: The payloads carried inside the single bundled file and unpacked to a per-user cache before the program runs. | **sourceNames**: IncludeNativeLibrariesForSelfExtract; IncludeAllContentForSelfExtract; DOTNET_BUNDLE_EXTRACT_BASE_DIR
- **term**: Release file name | **definition**: The public download name of a published artefact, formed as chatdbg-<platform key>-<platform triple><suffix>. | **sourceNames**: RELEASE_NAME; chatdbg-windows-win-x64.exe; chatdbg-linux-linux-x64
- **term**: Intermediate artefact | **definition**: The single-file upload handed from a platform build job to the publication job, retained 7 days. | **sourceNames**: actions/upload-artifact; chatdbg-<key>-binary; retention-days
- **term**: Release version | **definition**: The tag and title component of a published release: the operator's input verbatim, else 'v' plus the scraped project version. | **sourceNames**: release_version; github.event.inputs.version; VERSION
- **term**: Project version | **definition**: The 3-part version string scraped by text pattern out of the console-shell project descriptor, defaulting to 1.0.0 when unmatched. | **sourceNames**: PROJECT_VERSION; <Version>; grep -oP
- **term**: Publication credential | **definition**: The long-lived repository secret authorising creation of a public release. | **sourceNames**: GH_PATT; secrets.GH_PATT; personal access token
- **term**: Platform matrix row | **definition**: One entry defining an agent operating system, a platform triple, an executable suffix and a row key; two exist. | **sourceNames**: strategy.matrix.include; os; runtime; extension; platform
- **term**: Fan-in join | **definition**: The rule that the publication job runs only after every platform row succeeds, so a half-published release cannot exist. | **sourceNames**: needs: build; job dependency
- **term**: Fatal-error contract | **definition**: The single machine-readable signal a packaged artefact emits: exit code 0 on clean shutdown, or one line 'Fatal error: <message>' on standard output followed by exit code 1. | **sourceNames**: Program.cs catch block; Environment.Exit(1)
- **term**: Per-user state root | **definition**: One of the three well-known user directories the packaged artefact writes into, keeping the install location read-only. | **sourceNames**: Environment.SpecialFolder.UserProfile; LocalApplicationData; ApplicationData
- **term**: Native inference payload | **definition**: The large per-platform machine-learning backend library shipped inside the artefact; two mutually exclusive backends are referenced simultaneously. | **sourceNames**: LLamaSharp.Backend.Cpu; LLamaSharp.Backend.Cuda12; llama.dll; libllama.dll; libllama.so; <RID>/native/
- **term**: Identity metadata | **definition**: The version, title, description, company, product and copyright stamped into the artefact; kept by the bundled profile, stripped by the native profile. | **sourceNames**: GenerateAssemblyInfo; GenerateTargetFrameworkAttribute; AssemblyTitle; Company; Product; Copyright; Version; AssemblyVersion; FileVersion
- **term**: Full-screen terminal shell | **definition**: The windowed front end that paints a persistent menu bar, transcript, input frame, status line and modal dialogs over the whole terminal surface; one of the product's two alternative shells. | **sourceNames**: Terminal GUI Shell, ChatDbg.Shell.Gui, Xcaciv.ChatDbg.Shell.Gui
- **term**: Transcript | **definition**: The scrollable conversation view captioned 'Chat History', rebuilt from scratch on every refresh with one row per role banner and one per wrapped body line. | **sourceNames**: _chatView, chat frame, Chat History FrameView
- **term**: Token log probabilities | **definition**: Per-token confidence data returned by a model: a token, its log probability, its derived probability as a 0-1 fraction, and an ordered list of alternatives. | **sourceNames**: logprobs, LogProbabilities, TokenLogProbabilities
- **term**: Token probability panel | **definition**: The optional side panel captioned 'Token Probabilities' that lists every token of one selected assistant message with a colour heat map and its alternatives. | **sourceNames**: _logProbView, Token Probabilities FrameView, log probs panel
- **term**: Probability heat map | **definition**: Ten fixed colour buckets indexed by floor(probability x 10) clamped to 0-9, applied to token and alternative rows in the panel. | **sourceNames**: _probColorSchemes, GetProbabilityColorScheme
- **term**: Probability marker | **definition**: The clickable diamond glyph rendered at column 2 of any assistant message carrying probability data; clicking it points the panel at that message. | **sourceNames**: the U+25CA button created in RenderChatHistory
- **term**: Resting status text | **definition**: The default one-line context display reading 'Provider: <provider> | Model: <model id> | Prompt: <system prompt name>'. | **sourceNames**: UpdateStatusLabel, _statusLabel context string
- **term**: Transient status message | **definition**: A status-line message that replaces the resting text and is unconditionally restored to it 3000 ms later by an independent, uncancellable timer. | **sourceNames**: ShowStatusMessage
- **term**: Settings record | **definition**: The in-memory configuration object holding provider, model, temperature, token limits, probability display preferences, credential flag and local-model tuning; seeded from disk at startup and written on three explicit occasions. | **sourceNames**: ChatSettings, _settings
- **term**: Command registry | **definition**: The lowercase-name-to-command map built once at startup with exactly 15 entries and never mutated. | **sourceNames**: Dictionary<string, ICommand> commands
- **term**: Command result | **definition**: The value every command returns: a success flag, an optional message and an exit-requested flag. | **sourceNames**: CommandResult, SuccessResult, ErrorResult, ExitResult
- **term**: Provider key | **definition**: The lowercase identifier under which each model backend adapter is registered: azure, bedrock, llama. | **sourceNames**: aiServices dictionary keys, ChatSettings.Provider
- **term**: Prompt record | **definition**: A named, persisted instruction text with a description, a creation instant and an unused last-used instant; the name is the primary key and the file identity. | **sourceNames**: SystemPrompt, system prompt
- **term**: Demo visualisation | **definition**: The menu action that runs a command producing sample token-probability output through the rich-console formatter rather than in a window. | **sourceNames**: demologprobs, DemoLogProbsCommand, Run Demo Visualization
- **term**: Operating-system credential store | **definition**: The platform secret vault a named credential can be written to and read from under the target names ChatDbg:AzureApiKey, ChatDbg:AwsAccessKey and ChatDbg:AwsSecretKey. | **sourceNames**: Windows Credential Manager, WindowsCredentialManager, UseWindowsCredentialManager
- **term**: Bubble width factor | **definition**: The 3/4 multiplier that keeps a wrapped message body to three-quarters of the transcript's usable width after subtracting twice the 2-column padding. | **sourceNames**: ((width - 4) * 3) / 4 in WrapText
- **term**: Grid View Max Alternatives | **definition**: The user-visible setting bounding how many alternatives are displayed per token in the panel (1-20, default 5); despite its name it drives the list-form panel, not any grid. | **sourceNames**: GridViewMaxAlternatives
- **term**: Display Mode and Layout preferences | **definition**: Two persisted probability-display preferences (show-all-tokens versus samples; grid versus list) that the in-window panel ignores entirely. | **sourceNames**: ShowAllTokens, UseGridView
- **term**: Token | **definition**: A vocabulary fragment the model actually reads and emits, as opposed to a character. Reported with a zero-based sequence index, an integer vocabulary index, a display text and an approximate character span. | **sourceNames**: TokenInfo, token, LLamaToken
- **term**: Vocabulary index | **definition**: The integer identifier of a token within the model's vocabulary. Real for tokenization; synthetic (1000 + word index) on the inspection generation path; always -1 on chat-time analysis records. | **sourceNames**: TokenId, tokenId
- **term**: Token log probability | **definition**: The natural logarithm of the probability the model assigned to a token at a generation step. Displayed to 5 decimal places with a derived percentage (e^logprob x 100) to 2 decimal places. | **sourceNames**: LogProb, Logprob, logprob, SelectedLogProb
- **term**: Alternative (runner-up token) | **definition**: A token the model could have chosen instead at a given step, carrying its own text, vocabulary index and log probability. Emitted in strictly descending probability order. | **sourceNames**: TokenProbabilityAlternative, Alternatives, TopCandidates, TokenCandidate
- **term**: Alternatives-per-position count | **definition**: How many runner-up tokens are captured or fabricated at each generation step. Product default 5; range-checked 1-20 only where it is set, never where it is read. | **sourceNames**: logProbabilitiesTopK, LogProbabilitiesTopK, topK, --top
- **term**: Beginning-of-sequence marker | **definition**: A synthetic leading token prepended during tokenization; it is counted in the reported total but is given the sentinel character span -1/-1 and displayed as 'n/a'. | **sourceNames**: BOS, addBos, special token
- **term**: Character span | **definition**: The approximate inclusive start and exclusive end offsets of a token in the original text, produced by a uniform-distribution estimate rather than a real offset map. | **sourceNames**: CharStartPosition, CharEndPosition, Char Range
- **term**: Tokenization report | **definition**: The on-screen table of index, vocabulary index and token text produced by the tokenization command. | **sourceNames**: TokenizeCommand, Token analysis
- **term**: Full inspection | **definition**: The three-stage report (tokenization, then probability analysis, then attribution analysis) produced by the inspection command behind an interactive consent gate. | **sourceNames**: InspectCommand, /inspect
- **term**: Consent gate | **definition**: The blocking yes/no question asked between the cheap tokenization stage and the expensive generation stage; only 'y' or 'yes' proceeds, and anything else (including end-of-file) silently declines. | **sourceNames**: Console.ReadLine consent prompt in InspectCommand
- **term**: Attribution | **definition**: A per-generated-token claim about which span of input, or of earlier output, influenced it, with a numeric influence score. Produced by a fixed heuristic, not by gradients or attention. | **sourceNames**: TokenAttribution, AttributionMap, InfluencingText, InfluenceScore
- **term**: Influence score | **definition**: The numeric strength attached to an attribution entry. Always the constant 0.8, displayed as 0.80. | **sourceNames**: InfluenceScore
- **term**: Step-probability record | **definition**: The distribution at one generation step: position, selected token identity and log probability, plus the alternative list. | **sourceNames**: TokenProbabilityMap
- **term**: Per-step analysis record | **definition**: The structured, exportable object the inference subsystem writes for each generated token during ordinary chat: step, token text, vocabulary index, probability, log probability, prompt offset, candidate list, debug text and a model-state snapshot. The only persisted entity in this feature. | **sourceNames**: TokenAnalysis
- **term**: Model-state snapshot | **definition**: The context/state values captured alongside a per-step analysis record: tokens processed, context token count, context window size, remaining capacity, timestamp and a free-form diagnostic map. | **sourceNames**: ModelStateInfo, ModelState, modelState
- **term**: Prompt offset | **definition**: A field documented as naming which part of the prompt influenced a token, but written by the only producer as the step index. | **sourceNames**: PromptOffset, promptOffset
- **term**: Tokenization visualization block | **definition**: A fixed five-line explanatory block printed above the token detail table, which explicitly disclaims that real token boundaries cannot be shown. | **sourceNames**: Visualization, GenerateVisualization
- **term**: Local-model provider | **definition**: The configured provider value that gates both working commands; the exact lowercase string 'llama'. | **sourceNames**: Provider == "llama", LLamaSharp provider
- **term**: Context window size | **definition**: The token capacity of an inference context. This feature hard-codes 512 for tokenization loads and 2048 for generation loads, ignoring the user's configured value (default 4096). | **sourceNames**: ContextSize, llamaContextSize, ContextParams.ContextSize
- **term**: Generation budget | **definition**: The maximum number of tokens requested from the generator during inspection: the smaller of 10 and the configured maximum response length. | **sourceNames**: numTokensToGenerate, Math.Min(10, MaxTokens)
- **term**: Diagnostic log sink | **definition**: The in-memory, lock-guarded buffer that timestamps and levels native inference-library log lines and flushes them to a daily file past a character threshold. | **sourceNames**: LLamaSharpLogConfig
- **term**: Local inference back end | **definition**: The in-process model back end that runs an open-weights model from a local weights file with no network call, no account and no credential; one of three interchangeable back ends, registered under the provider key 'llama'. | **sourceNames**: LLamaSharpService, IAIService implementation, provider key "llama"
- **term**: Model weights file | **definition**: A single quantised model file on local disk; for this back end the 'model identifier' setting is this file's path rather than a remote model name. | **sourceNames**: ChatSettings.ModelId, GGUF file, LLamaWeights
- **term**: Inference context | **definition**: A sized working state created over the loaded weights that holds the model's per-session computation state; its size is the context window. | **sourceNames**: LLamaContext, ContextSize, llamaContextSize
- **term**: Engine-side chat session | **definition**: The conversation state retained inside the inference engine, seeded once at load time with a system-role message; this — not the product's message list — is where multi-turn continuity actually lives. | **sourceNames**: ChatSession, InteractiveExecutor, LLama.Common.ChatHistory
- **term**: Text piece | **definition**: The unit the engine streams back: a decoded fragment of text, not necessarily one model token. All per-token counters, records and probability lists in this feature count pieces. | **sourceNames**: streamed string chunk from ChatSession.ChatAsync, tokenCounter
- **term**: Token log probability | **definition**: The natural logarithm of a token's probability, stored per generated piece and exponentiated by the display layer to render a confidence value. | **sourceNames**: logprob, LogProb, TokenLogProbabilities.LogProb
- **term**: Alternatives count | **definition**: The setting naming how many alternative tokens to capture per position; also silently used as the sampler's selection limit on both generation paths. | **sourceNames**: logProbabilitiesTopK, LogProbabilitiesTopK, top-k, TopK
- **term**: Candidate token | **definition**: One of the alternative tokens the model considered at a position, carrying its text (or an 'id:<n>' placeholder) and a within-selection log probability. | **sourceNames**: CandidateToken, topCandidates, top_alternatives, TokenLogProbability alternatives
- **term**: Score vector | **definition**: The engine's raw per-vocabulary scores for the next position, read through a run-time name lookup and transformed into candidate probabilities. | **sourceNames**: logits, GetLogits
- **term**: Normalised-exponential transform over selected scores | **definition**: The softmax applied to only the selected top candidates rather than the whole vocabulary, so returned candidate probabilities always sum to 1.0. | **sourceNames**: softmax over topK logits, numerically stabilised softmax
- **term**: Per-token analysis record | **definition**: The exportable per-step introspection record carrying step index, token text, a temperature-derived probability estimate, prompt offset, candidates, a debug sentence and a model-state snapshot. | **sourceNames**: TokenAnalysis
- **term**: Model-state snapshot | **definition**: The per-step context-utilisation record reporting tokens processed, context token count, context size, remaining context, a timestamp and a three-entry debug map. | **sourceNames**: ModelState, TokenAnalysis.ModelState, debugInfo
- **term**: Temperature-derived confidence estimate | **definition**: The six-step lookup table mapping the configured sampling temperature to a fixed 'probability' written into every analysis record of a run, in place of a measured value. | **sourceNames**: EstimateProbabilityFromTemperature, probability field of TokenAnalysis
- **term**: Introspection path / fast path | **definition**: The two mutually exclusive generation routines: the introspection path produces per-piece probability and analysis records; the fast path streams and concatenates text only and returns an absent probability list. | **sourceNames**: sampling pipeline mode, standard ChatSession mode, GenerateWithSamplingPipeline / GenerateStandard
- **term**: Engine log capture | **definition**: The armed-once redirection of the inference engine's own native log output into a timestamped in-memory buffer that is periodically appended to a daily log file. | **sourceNames**: LLamaSharpLogConfig, NativeLogConfig, native log callback
- **term**: Daily log file | **definition**: The append-only file named 'llamasharp_<yyyyMMdd>.log' in the log directory to which the log buffer is flushed; no size rotation, retention limit or pruning exists. | **sourceNames**: llamasharp_{date}.log, LogDirectory
- **term**: Process-wide generation lock / model-load lock | **definition**: Two separate process-wide binary locks that serialise, respectively, all generation and all model loading across every instance in the process; neither has a timeout or cancellation. | **sourceNames**: static SemaphoreSlim _generationLock, static SemaphoreSlim _modelLoadLock
- **term**: Stop sequence | **definition**: A literal string whose emission halts generation; hard-coded here to exactly 'User:' and 'USER:'. | **sourceNames**: AntiPrompts, InferenceParams.AntiPrompts
- **term**: Graphics-layer offload count | **definition**: How many model layers are moved onto the graphics processor; 0 means processor-only. | **sourceNames**: llamaGpuLayers, llamaGpuLayerCount, GpuLayerCount
- **term**: Maximum new tokens | **definition**: The per-turn generation budget, falling back to 512 when the configured value is not positive. | **sourceNames**: maxTokens, MaxTokens, InferenceParams.MaxTokens

---

## External technology rows

- **capability**: Hosted, deployment-scoped large-language-model chat-completion service | **protocol**: HTTPS + JSON; route /openai/deployments/{deployment}/chat/completions?api-version=...; header auth via a header named api-key | **sourceUsed**: Azure OpenAI Service, API version literal 2023-12-01-preview | **notes**: Model identifier IS the deployment name. API version hard-coded, not configurable, and is a preview version. Request fields: messages[{role,content}], temperature, max_tokens, top_p, logprobs, top_logprobs. Response fields read: choices[], choices[0].message.content, top-level logprobs (object-with-content or array), choices[0].logprobs, per-token token/logprob/top_logprobs[]/top_alternatives[]. INFERRED: the wire contract is read from source only, never asserted by a test or exercised live; re-verify against current provider documentation.
- **capability**: Vendor client library for the same hosted service, used only on the non-log-probability path | **protocol**: same service, library-managed wire format | **sourceUsed**: Azure.AI.OpenAI 2.1.0 with its bundled OpenAI chat-client types | **notes**: Only the temperature option is set through it. A clone may use one direct request path for both cases, but must then consciously decide whether to preserve the maximum-tokens asymmetry (QUIRK-6.3) and the role-filtering asymmetry (QUIRK-6.5).
